## 0. How the coding agent must use this file

- **Do not treat previous completion reports as proof.** This audit is based on the actual uploaded source tree; repository-reported test numbers are evidence only unless reproduced from this exact tree.
- **Every archive file is listed in §9 with a `#` comment.** A targeted file gets exact implementation instructions; every other file gets a preservation/static-scan instruction and must not be skipped.
- Implement in priority order **P0 → P1 → P2**, then run the full verification matrix in §8.
- Do not fabricate GLO data, government affiliation, payment completion, prize authenticity, support contacts, campaigns, or provider settlement state.
- For money: **exact decimal strings + explicit currency + DB transaction/row lock + idempotency + ledger proof**. No float arithmetic for currency.

## 1. Current artifact evidence

- Source tree contains **1640 files** and about **422067 text lines** in readable files (counted, not claimed to be human-read character-by-character).
- The exact uploaded ZIP does **not** contain `vendor/autoload.php` and does **not** contain `public/build/manifest.json`; therefore a fresh Laravel runtime/test/build proof cannot be claimed from this archive alone.
- `vendor/` and `public/build/` must be produced by the release pipeline; the absence is a release-artifact issue, not permission to skip tests.
- Rust CI is present in `.github/workflows/ci.yml`, but this audit environment does not have `cargo`/`rustc`; Rust execution was not independently reproduced here.
- PHP/JS/etc. syntax should be rerun in the implementation environment after changes; no current artifact claim should be promoted to production proof until the exact rebuild passes.

## 2. GLO official-reference comparison (fact layer)

| GLO reference | Current code | Audit disposition |
|---|---|---|
| Standard printed L6 ticket is **80 THB single ticket** | `config/glo.php` still declares `sold_in_pairs=true`, `pair_set_value=120.00` | **P1 GAP — fix config/admin copy/tests** |
| L6 ladder totals **14,168 prizes / 48,000,000 THB** at full sell-out | `config/glo.php` + calculator match these headline totals | **MATCH — preserve** |
| Unsold tickets reduce prize amounts proportionally | Calculator implements proportional allocation, but claim settlement still reads fixed configured tier amount | **P1 GAP — wire calculator into claim payment** |
| Government lottery prize claim: **1 THB stamp duty per 200 THB (or fraction)** | `GloStampDutyCalculator` matches; `config/glo.php` has contradictory `tax_withheld=true` on ordinary GLO tiers | **P1 GAP — normalize rule config** |
| Claim within **2 years**; under-20 cannot claim | `config/glo.php` + claim service enforce these gates | **MATCH — preserve** |
| GLO publishes official result checking and historical statistics | App has result lanes/importers, but real approved production source/payload still needs operational population | **P1 OPS/DATA GAP** |

## 3. P0 — production blockers

### P0-01 — Legal/operator identity and authorization
- `config/legal.php` + public footer/legal pages: keep a single approved operator/legal identity. Do not present the platform as GLO/government-owned unless there is actual authorization/evidence.
- This is a business/legal gate, not something source code can manufacture. The coding agent must prevent contradictory branding and add regression tests for government-endorsement wording.

### P0-02 — Real-money release artifact
- Exact uploaded ZIP lacks `vendor/autoload.php` and `public/build/manifest.json`. Create a deterministic release bundle after dependency install + asset build; checksum it; run smoke tests against the exact bundle.

### P0-03 — Payment rails must be truthful before enabling automatic money movement
- bKash/Nagad/Crypto currently report `FullyImplemented` while their withdrawal paths are pending/manual/synthetic. They must be implemented end-to-end or marked unavailable/manual and excluded from automatic capability.

### P0-04 — GLO L6 settlement must use proportional unsold calculation
- A proportional calculator exists, but `GloPrizeClaimService::configuredWin()` uses fixed config prize amounts. The claim/payment path must consume the authoritative draw sale allocation before money is paid.

## 4. P1 — real functional/security/business gaps

### P1-01 — Responsible Gaming split-brain
- **Evidence:** `app/Http/Controllers/Web/PlayerWebController.php:619-632` writes through `App\Services\Security\ResponsibleGamingService`, while canonical enforcement reads versioned limits via `ResponsibleGamingLimitService`. A web-set limit can therefore be invisible to the enforcement gate.
- **Implementation:** Unify writes behind versioned service or a synchronized adapter; prove with web→bet/deposit tests.

### P1-02 — Responsible Gaming currency scope
- **Evidence:** `ResponsibleGamingLimitService::bindingLimitFor()` filters user/type/status but not currency; enforcement deposit/loss queries also do not consistently scope currency.
- **Implementation:** Bind every RG limit to wallet/operation currency and test THB/USD/BDT isolation.

### P1-03 — Wallet summary can mix currencies
- **Evidence:** `PlayerWebController::wallet()` sums deposits/payouts by user without currency filter, then formats them using one selected wallet currency.
- **Implementation:** Aggregate by selected wallet currency or show per-currency summaries; no mixed-currency totals under one symbol.

### P1-04 — Web deposit/withdraw are THB-only while payment config exposes BDT/USD rails
- **Evidence:** Web controller explicitly resolves THB wallet and constructs THB Money, while payment config supports BDT/USD methods.
- **Implementation:** Choose one explicit product contract: THB-only web finance with non-THB rails disabled/hidden, or implement currency selection + wallet routing + limit scope.

### P1-05 — Player finance UI still config-only method list
- **Evidence:** `availableDepositMethods()` / `availableWithdrawalMethods()` only call `methodOptions(config(...allowed_methods))`.
- **Implementation:** Use gateway capability + currency checks in GET UI and POST validation so the UI never advertises a method initiation will reject.

### P1-06 — bKash withdrawal is synthetic pending
- **Evidence:** `BkashGateway::initiateWithdrawal()` returns local `BKASH-B2C-*` pending reference with no provider API call.
- **Implementation:** Implement real B2C disbursement/status/webhook or mark unsupported/manual and remove FullyImplemented.

### P1-07 — Nagad withdrawal is manual placeholder
- **Evidence:** `NagadGateway::initiateWithdrawal()` returns `NAGAD-DISBURSE-*` pending + manual metadata, yet status is FullyImplemented.
- **Implementation:** Same maturity correction; require provider settlement evidence before automatic capability.

### P1-08 — Crypto rail uses synthetic local transaction identity
- **Evidence:** `CryptoGateway::initiateDeposit/Withdrawal()` generates local invoice/tx IDs rather than a real external processor transaction.
- **Implementation:** Integrate a real processor/address monitoring path or make it manual/disabled; webhook alone must not make a fake local transaction official.

### P1-09 — Financial web POST routes are not throttled
- **Evidence:** `routes/web.php` authenticated group currently puts `/bet/purchase`, `/deposit`, `/withdraw` under `auth` but not named `throttle:*`. Named limiters exist in `AppServiceProvider`.
- **Implementation:** Attach user-scoped rate limiters + financial-critical limiter; test 429 behavior and legitimate retries.

### P1-10 — Rust input ceiling is enforced after full read
- **Evidence:** `security/weekly-result-integrity/src/main.rs` calls `read_to_string()` before checking 64 KiB.
- **Implementation:** Use bounded reader/`take(MAX+1)` before allocation; add oversized-stream test.

### P1-11 — Laravel 11 security support ended
- **Evidence:** `composer.lock` locks `laravel/framework` v11.56.1. Official Laravel support matrix lists Laravel 11 security fixes through 2026-03-12 and Laravel 12 through 2027-02-24.
- **Implementation:** Upgrade to a supported Laravel line (12/13 as compatible) with lockfile, regression tests, and dependency audit.

### P1-12 — GLO L6 ticket pair config is obsolete
- **Evidence:** `config/glo.php` says `sold_in_pairs=true` + `pair_set_value=120.00`; GLO’s current public site states the printed L6 format is a single 80-THB ticket.
- **Implementation:** Fix config, admin view, docs and tests to the current single-ticket model.

### P1-13 — GLO prize tax fields contradict the official rule note
- **Evidence:** `config/glo.php` note says GLO income-tax exempt + stamp duty, but adjacent/second/third/fourth/fifth entries set `tax_withheld=true`.
- **Implementation:** Normalize ordinary GLO L6 tiers to the approved tax/duty source; keep charity lottery rules separate if ever supported.

### P1-14 — Historical result payloads are still an operational dependency
- **Evidence:** Importer architecture exists but approved real payloads/source feed are not bundled by default.
- **Implementation:** Before public result marketing, import/reconcile real National/Weekly/Mega/PCSO history with provenance/checksum/version.

### P1-15 — Result provenance must never imply “official” from data shape alone
- **Evidence:** Public result code must use explicit stored source state; unknown/unverified/internal/fixture rows must not get an Official label.
- **Implementation:** Add allow-list provenance state and render tests.

### P1-16 — Results page has transport/business logic in Blade
- **Evidence:** `resources/views/results/index.blade.php` queries/shapes result data in the view.
- **Implementation:** Move to controller/service/DTO and keep Blade presentation-only.

### P1-17 — Legal page pipeline drift
- **Evidence:** Privacy is a route/view separate from the canonical legal service and has its own copy/date behavior; duplicate static Terms also exists.
- **Implementation:** Use one versioned legal content source and one metadata pipeline.

### P1-18 — Player-facing identity fallback risk
- **Evidence:** Any dashboard/profile fallback to sample names/emails/IDs must be removed.
- **Implementation:** Render authenticated user identity or a neutral unavailable state; add leak test.

### P1-19 — Deposit/withdraw RG and finance must share currency context
- **Evidence:** `assertDepositAllowed`/loss calculations are amount-only while payment/wallet layer is currency-specific.
- **Implementation:** Pass Currency through RG enforcement and ensure the queried evidence ledger matches it.

## 5. P2 — other gaps / marketing / UX / maintainability

### P2-01 — Hardcoded user-facing English remains on financial/profile views
- **Implementation:** Move visible button/status/error/helper copy to EN/TH translation namespaces; also localize request validation messages.

### P2-02 — No discoverable locale control in shared navigation
- **Implementation:** `/locale/{locale}` and middleware exist, but add a visible language switch and persistence test; do not rely only on query URL.

### P2-03 — Dashboard display should not silently hardcode business multipliers
- **Implementation:** Use canonical payout rule service and explicit unavailable state; format through Money/currency where monetary.

### P2-04 — Navigation still has multiple sources of truth
- **Implementation:** Consolidate route/name/order/label-key inventory while preserving auth/authorization boundaries.

### P2-05 — Home marketing configuration is mostly optional/empty
- **Implementation:** Production must populate only approved real campaigns/support/app/agent links; add expiry/host validation and no-fake-data tests.

### P2-06 — Payment visuals/status should match real capabilities
- **Implementation:** Do not show a provider logo/CTA when the rail is disabled, manual-only, wrong currency, or immature.

### P2-07 — Home “VERIFIED” semantics need precision
- **Implementation:** Use `DATABASE`, `INTERNAL`, `OFFICIAL_SOURCE_VERIFIED`, etc. according to actual evidence.

### P2-08 — PWA is present but release artifact must contain its manifest/service worker
- **Implementation:** Build and smoke-test `/manifest.webmanifest` + `/sw.js`; only show install UX when files exist.

### P2-09 — SEO/content must be source-aware
- **Implementation:** Canonical/meta/OG copy must not claim government affiliation or official certification without evidence.

### P2-10 — Real support/contact/app links need operational ownership
- **Implementation:** Configure only verified endpoints; add monitoring/checks for broken destinations.

### P2-11 — CI should prove release bundle completeness
- **Implementation:** Add vendor/build/Rust binary/.env/security/dependency checks to release workflow.

## 6. Security / wallet / Rust release gates

- **Wallet:** user + wallet + currency must be part of every finance operation identity; lock the exact wallet row before debit/credit; ledger entry and balance mutation must commit atomically; idempotency must reject a different request reusing the same key.
- **Withdrawal:** reserve/hold funds before payout; provider state must be externally verifiable for automatic rails; manual rails must remain visibly manual and require operator settlement evidence.
- **Deposit:** credit only after verified provider webhook/status or approved manual reconciliation; browser return URLs must never change payment state.
- **RG:** exclusion blocks play/deposit but must not trap a player’s withdrawal; limits must be currency-scoped and versioned; increases cool off, stricter changes bind immediately.
- **Rust:** bounded input, deterministic canonicalization, `unsafe_code` forbidden, no network/DB dependencies, signed/authenticated source claims kept separate from hash-only integrity.
- **Web security:** add named rate limiters to money POSTs, enforce HTTPS in actual middleware/proxy, keep CSRF active, require active user/wallet, and audit every failed/blocked money attempt.
- **Dependency security:** upgrade unsupported framework line before release and run locked dependency/security scans.

## 7. Page / business surface matrix — do not skip

| Surface | Code family | Required final state |
|---|---|---|
| Home | `/`, HomePageDataService, home components | Real next draw/result/provenance, truthful marketing, actual payment/support/app links, locale + SEO; no government claim unless authorized. |
| Results | `/results` + result services | Controller/service-owned data, explicit provenance, published data only, no fake sample API/ticket language. |
| Ticket Checker | `/check` | Anti-enumeration, rate limits, safe verification, no authenticity claim without authoritative evidence. |
| Sales Points | `/sales-points` | Real/approved locations only; no fabricated store data. |
| About/Vision | `/about`, `/vision` | Operator/legal identity consistent with approved source. |
| Terms/Fees/Discounts | `/terms`, `/fees`, `/discounts` | Single legal/business source of truth; approved price/rule table; no legacy stale values. |
| Privacy | `/privacy` | Versioned legal content/effective date. |
| Contact | `/contact` | Real support destination; spam/rate limit; no fake contact information. |
| National Lottery | `/national-lottery*` | Real approved source/import, year/archive consistency, published-only sitemap. |
| Weekly Lottery | `/weekly-lottery*` | Same + Rust verifier required for the configured integrity policy. |
| Mega/Bingo | `/bingo-lottery*` | Explicit naming parity and approved source/provenance. |
| PCSO | `/pcso-lottery*` | Approved source/provenance; no fixture publication. |
| Login/Register/Reset | guest auth routes | Throttle, password policy, CSRF, account audit, supported framework. |
| Dashboard/Draws | authenticated player | Currency-aware data, no fabricated identity/multiplier values. |
| Bet | `/bet`, `/bet/purchase` | Browser purchase transport, CSRF, idempotency, rate limit, RG, atomic wallet/ledger. |
| Bets history | `/bets` | Exact currency, settlement status, no client-controlled payout. |
| Wallet | `/wallet` | Single-currency summary or grouped currencies; exact Money; immutable ledger. |
| Deposit | `/deposit` | Actual capable rails only; currency-compatible wallet; verified settlement. |
| Withdraw | `/withdraw` | Actual capable rails only; canonical destination validation; reserve/approval/provider evidence. |
| Profile/RG | `/profile`, `/profile/limits` | Versioned RG service, cooldown/active state, localized validation. |
| Account verification/grade | `/account/*` | Real KYC/evidence workflows; no sample identities. |
| Payment callbacks | `/payment/*` | Return pages presentation-only; state changes only from verified provider events. |
| Legacy `.php` bridge | `LegacyRedirectController` | Every known legacy URL → correct modern 301; unknown `.php` → 404; preserve canonical host. |
| Sitemap/Robots/PWA | SEO assets | Only published public data; release artifact has manifest/service worker; correct canonical/noindex rules. |
| Admin/GLO claim pages | Filament + GLO services | Separate reference/authorized data from operator data; all money actions audited; no “official” shortcut. |

## 8. Mandatory final verification matrix

- `composer install --no-dev` or production-equivalent deterministic install from lockfile; then verify `vendor/autoload.php` exists.
- `npm ci && npm run build`; verify `public/build/manifest.json` and all referenced assets exist.
- PHP syntax/lint over **all PHP files**; JS syntax checks over all JS; JSON/CSS structural checks.
- Full PHPUnit/Laravel test suite from the exact rebuilt artifact; record passed/skipped/failed + assertions.
- Rust: `cargo fmt --check`, `cargo check --locked --all-targets`, `cargo test --locked`, `cargo build --release --locked`; bounded-input smoke tests.
- Wallet concurrency tests: concurrent same-user bet/deposit/withdraw, double-spend prevention, idempotency replay and mixed-currency isolation.
- Payment tests: capability vs UI parity; supported-currency parity; provider webhooks; payout/manual-state reconciliation; synthetic-reference rejection for automatic rails.
- RG tests: web/API parity, version binding, cooldown, stricter-now, currency isolation, exclusion behavior.
- GLO tests: L6 80 THB single-ticket model; 14,168/48m full allocation; unsold proportional claim settlement; 1/200 stamp duty; 2-year claim window; age 20+; provenance.
- Route smoke crawl: every public route, authenticated route, payment callback and legacy bridge mapping.
- Production artifact scan: no `.env`, secrets, debug mode, placeholder/sample identity, fake marketing data, raw API demo URLs, or unsupported payment driver marked automatic.
- Live cutover crawl only after release bundle is proven; verify each page/legacy URL against canonical modern destination and compare current result/fees/legal copy with approved source-of-truth.

## 10. Definition of done

- No P0 or P1 finding remains unresolved.
- No payment driver is advertised as automatic/fully implemented unless real provider settlement is implemented and independently tested.
- No RG write path can create a limit that the canonical enforcement service ignores.
- No wallet/ledger/RG query mixes currencies.
- GLO L6 product configuration matches the current single-ticket 80 THB reference model, and unsold proportional payout is wired into actual claim settlement.
- Rust verifier is bounded before reading, tested, released, and packaged; hash-only integrity is never mislabeled as source authenticity.
- Supported Laravel security line, deterministic dependencies, built assets and vendor tree are present in the release bundle.
- Every file has been reviewed against its `#` instruction and the final regression suite is green from the exact release artifact.
